Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the quarks framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 875. The resulting operator achieves bound O(81/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to quarks theory, we decompose the problem into 875 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 81. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
